# ASSET INVENTORY — Tahu Bulat APK

**Target:** `com.owngames.tahubulat.293.arm64.apk`  
**Package:** com.owngames.tahubulat  
**Version:** 15.17.6 (versionCode 293)  
**APK SHA-256:** `600bfe441a1f63f08d7bfcfa643316aa09fb1919f54ba83c0bb2147af5295f5a`  
**REA evidence:** `ev_cfbce936dd36f212f4f8ab074a4080d475540e2081e0c9cba7837778f1dea06f` (outer), `ev_4150bbd787be08058021c75d9f473b3809c93ea15c60b58c802d2d2bf763695d` (base split)

**Extraction flow:** REA `open_binary` → `inspect_artifact` → `extract_artifact` (outer App Bundle) → `open_binary` + `inspect_artifact` + `extract_artifact` on `assets/splits/com.owngames.tahubulat.apk`.

**Encoding:** engine payload `.oga/.ogt/.ogf` is XOR 0x7F; decoded losslessly to PNG/XML/OTF. No re-compression, no resizing, no merging.

**Totals:** 2,212 images (PNG) + 402 layout/animation XML + 7 fonts = **2,621 visual resources**. 198 audio files were found but are outside visual scope.

## Notes / Limitations

- APK is an Android App Bundle wrapper: the real game payload is in nested split `assets/splits/com.owngames.tahubulat.apk`; architecture/DPI/locale splits contain no game art.
- All art is stored as individual PNGs, not packed sprite atlases. No combined sprite sheets exist in the payload, so no frames were split or merged.
- Engine packs original UI atlas metadata (`.ogt` XML) using logical paths like `UI_baru/banner_loker.png`, but packed image filenames are engine-randomized. Static mapping from hashed pack filename → original logical path is not present in shipped assets; images are preserved under their packed names.
- `.ogt` is a mixed container: 402 XOR-XML files, 120 WAV, 10 MP3/ID3, 1 PNG. Only visual payloads were extracted.
- `.ogm` (68) are audio (MP3/OGG), `.oga` are PNG images, `.ogf` are OTTO fonts.
- No visual asset failed decoding. Zero corrupt PNGs after decode (2,212/2,212 verified).
- Character animation sets are individual frames; inventory marks `frame i/N` per source sequence.
